</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000532" name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84C_eus-gaap--BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zKSldx1pJW73" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_869_ziTvg4xJrOf9">Basis of Presentation</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The accompanying audited financial statements are presented in U.S. Dollars and in conformity the U.S. GAAP and pursuant to the rules and regulations of the SEC. Accordingly, they include all of the information and footnotes required by the U.S. GAAP. In the opinion of management, all adjustments (consisting of normal accruals) considered for a fair presentation have been included. They should be read in conjunction with the Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form 10-K, as filed with the SEC on February 7, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>




</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000534" name="BKHAU:EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_ecustom--EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock_zUokJUoDkVok" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_860_zT5cWw89DRQ3">Emerging Growth Company</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company is an &#8220;emerging growth company,&#8221; as defined in Section&#160;2(a) of the Securities Act, as modified by the Jumpstart Our Business Startups Act of 2012 (the &#8220;JOBS Act&#8221;), and it may take advantage of certain exemptions from various reporting requirements that are applicable to other public companies that are not emerging growth companies including, but not limited to, not being required to comply with the independent registered public accounting firm attestation requirements of Section&#160;404 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act, reduced disclosure obligations regarding executive compensation in its periodic reports and proxy statements, and exemptions from the requirements of holding a nonbinding advisory vote on executive compensation and shareholder approval of any golden parachute payments not previously approved.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Further, Section&#160;102(b)(1) of the JOBS Act exempts emerging growth companies from being required to comply with new or revised financial accounting standards until private companies (that is, those that have not had a Securities Act registration statement declared effective or do not have a class of securities registered under the Exchange Act) are required to comply with the new or revised financial accounting standards. The JOBS Act provides that a company can elect to opt out of the extended transition period and comply with the requirements that apply to non-emerging growth companies but any such election to opt out is irrevocable. The Company has elected not to opt out of such extended transition period which means that when a standard is issued or revised and it has different application dates for public or private companies, the Company, as an emerging growth company, can adopt the new or revised standard at the time private companies adopt the new or revised standard. This may make comparison of the Company&#8217;s financial statements with another public company that is neither an emerging growth company nor an emerging growth company that has opted out of using the extended transition period difficult or impossible because of the potential differences in accounting standards used.</p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_235_zgkhrN1gj5ca" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_237_zepjSADROcBg" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000538" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_84A_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_zIgwKlxahvP6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_866_ztKULMhLfJN9">Use of Estimates</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">In preparing these financial statements in conformity with U.S. GAAP, the Company&#8217;s management makes estimates and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the reported expenses during the reporting period.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Making estimates requires management to exercise significant judgment. It is at least reasonably possible that the estimate of the effect of a condition, situation or set of circumstances that existed at the date of the financial statements, which management considered in formulating its estimate, could change in the near term due to one or more future confirming events. Accordingly, the actual results could differ significantly from those estimates.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000540" name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock_zZmbrrf6uWSb" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_86C_zdSz6o45eOCl">Cash and Cash Equivalents</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company considers all short-term investments with an original maturity of three months or less when purchased to be cash equivalents. The Company had $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--CashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_pp0p0_c20251130__us-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAxis__us-gaap--CashEquivalentsMember_zSmGg995eI8d" title="Cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30_us-gaap_CashEquivalentsMember" id="Fact000542" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">39,521</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--CashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_pp0p0_c20241130__us-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAxis__us-gaap--CashEquivalentsMember_zPthLDaptwXd" title="Cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-30_us-gaap_CashEquivalentsMember" id="Fact000544" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">264,842</ix:nonFraction></span> in cash and <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_iI_pp0p0_dn_c20251130_zD7GXU1Blwb5" title="Cash and cash equivalents"><span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue_iI_pp0p0_dn_c20241130_zqpxUjBTcW85" title="Cash and cash equivalents"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000546" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsAtCarryingValue" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-30" id="Fact000548" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">none</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> in cash equivalents as of November 30, 2025 and 2024, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>


</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000550" name="BKHAU:InvestmenstHeldInTrustAccountPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84A_ecustom--InvestmenstHeldInTrustAccountPolicyTextBlock_zuTvZD7USCYc" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_863_zRwCDb1Ihjn1">Investments Held in Trust Account</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">As of November 30, 2025 and 2024, the Company had $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_pp0p0_c20251130_zSoF4ICNq8jc" title="Investments held in Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000552" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">23,827,149</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_iI_pp0p0_dn_c20241130_zZpGmnue5af5" title="Investments held in Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-30" id="Fact000554" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">71,829,264</ix:nonFraction></span> in investment held in the Trust Account comprised of money market funds that invest in U.S. government securities, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Investments in money market funds are presented on the balance sheets at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Earnings on investments held in the Trust Account are included in interest earned on investments held in the Trust Account in the accompanying statement of operations. The estimated fair value of investments held in the Trust Account is determined using available market information.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000556" name="BKHAU:DeferredOfferingCostPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_848_ecustom--DeferredOfferingCostPolicyTextBlock_z3TgO0pWUy6c" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_860_zyAAvtHgeFoi">Deferred Offering Costs</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company complies with the requirements of FASB ASC Topic 340-10-S99-1, &#8220;Other Assets and Deferred Costs &#8211; SEC Materials&#8221; (&#8220;ASC 340-10-S99&#8221;) and SEC Staff Accounting Bulletin Topic 5A, &#8220;Expenses of Offering&#8221;. Deferred offering costs were $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DeferredCosts_pp0p0_c20251130_zRRfmKufxEn8" title="Deferred cost"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredCosts" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000558" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">4,309,493</ix:nonFraction></span> consisting principally of $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--UnderwritingFees_pp0p0_c20241201__20251130_zwaNFCygVsL3" title="Underwriting fees"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:UnderwritingFees" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000560" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">3,795,000</ix:nonFraction></span> underwriting fees $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--LegalFees_pp0p0_c20241201__20251130_z3kKN9DXYKdh" title="Legal and other expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LegalFees" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000562" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">514,493</ix:nonFraction></span> legal and other expenses that are directly related to the IPO and charged to shareholders&#8217; equity upon the completion of the IPO.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000564" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_847_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock_zGCi3DeugDX6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_866_zKS6wrtKylZe">Income Taxes</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company follows the asset and liability method of accounting for income taxes under ASC 740, &#8220;Income Taxes.&#8221; Deferred tax assets and liabilities are recognized for the estimated future tax consequences attributable to differences between the financial statements carrying amounts of existing assets and liabilities and their respective tax bases. Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured using enacted tax rates expected to apply to taxable income in the years in which those temporary differences are expected to be recovered or settled. The effect on deferred tax assets and liabilities of a change in tax rates is recognized in income in the period that is included in the enactment date. Valuation allowances are established, when necessary, to reduce deferred tax assets to the amount expected to be realized. ASC 740 prescribes a recognition threshold and a measurement attribute for the financial statement recognition and measurement of tax positions taken or expected to be taken in a tax return. For those benefits to be recognized, a tax position must be more likely than not to be sustained upon examination by taxing authorities. The Company recognizes accrued interest and penalties related to unrecognized tax benefits as income tax expense. There was <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefits_iI_pp0p0_do_c20251130_zApCpd0pLUGi" title="Unrecognized tax benefits"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefits" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000566" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></span> unrecognized tax benefits, and <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued_iI_pp0p0_do_c20251130_zK4x6vpFBPta" title="Accrued for interest and penalties"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000568" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></span> amounts accrued for interest and penalties as of November 30, 2025. The Company is currently not aware of any issues under review that could result in significant payments, accruals or material deviation from its position.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">There is currently no taxation imposed on income by the Government of the Cayman Islands. In accordance with Cayman income tax regulations, income taxes are not levied on the Company. Consequently, income taxes are not reflected in the Company&#8217;s consolidated financial statements.</p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23F_z6RldxX16MIb" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000572" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_848_eus-gaap--EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock_zuzcpKqLt5b2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_861_zxJJOFl0WRh2">Net Income (Loss) Per Ordinary Share</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company complies with the accounting and disclosure requirements of FASB ASC 260, Earnings Per Share. Net income per ordinary is computed by dividing net income (loss) by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding during the period, excluding ordinary shares subject to forfeiture by the Initial Shareholders. As of November 30, 2025 and 2024, the Company did not have any dilutive securities and other contracts that could, potentially, be exercised or converted into ordinary shares and then share in the earnings of the Company. As a result, diluted income (loss) per ordinary share is the same as basic income (loss) per ordinary share for the periods presented.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The calculation of diluted income per ordinary
share does not consider the effect of the rights issued in connection with the (i) IPO, and (ii) the private placement, since the issuance
of the shares included in the rights is contingent upon the occurrence of future events. The rights entitle the holder to receive one-fifth
of one ordinary share representing a total of <span id="xdx_902_ecustom--OrdinaryShares_iI_c20251130_zLAZXSWrPob7" title="Ordinary shares"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:OrdinaryShares" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000574" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,427,100</ix:nonFraction></span> ordinary shares.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000608" name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk"><p id="xdx_846_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk_zCz4c4G9oZq5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_862_zNatAELTwBKb">Concentration of Credit Risk</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Financial instruments that potentially subject the Company to concentrations of credit risk consist of a cash account in a financial institution, which, at times, may exceed the Federal Depository Insurance Coverage of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--CashFDICInsuredAmount_pp0p0_c20251130_zAMxBADSgDU9" title="FDIC Coverage limit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashFDICInsuredAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000610" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. The Company has not experienced losses on this account and management believes the Company is not exposed to significant risks on such account as of November 30, 2025 and 2024.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000612" name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy_zECQ1wk6P138" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_865_zGeugIOjaj04">Fair Value of Financial Instruments</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under ASC 825, &#8220;Financial Instruments,&#8221; approximates the carrying amounts represented in the accompanying balance sheet, primarily due to their short-term nature.</p>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000614" name="BKHAU:RelatedPartyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_840_ecustom--RelatedPartyPolicyTextBlock_zxctpPmwI2e4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_86E_zvvcgcN8l2T">Related Party</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Parties, which can be a corporation or individual,
are considered to be related if the Company has the ability, directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise significant
influence over the other party in making financial and operational decisions. Companies are also considered to be related if they are
subject to common control or common significant influence.</p>



<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23E_zubdLGyOMLEj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>



<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 65; Value: 1 -->
    <div id="xdx_235_zAZ6jVtdMHm9" style="border-bottom: Black 2pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">F-<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->14<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div>
    <div id="xdx_235_zVjUIQrHW74b" style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>


<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_234_zW4IW8vMlUnl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000618" name="BKHAU:OrdinarySharesSubjectToPossibleRedemptionPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_847_ecustom--OrdinarySharesSubjectToPossibleRedemptionPolicyTextBlock_zSdbXu8dl08" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Ordinary Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for its ordinary shares subject to possible redemption in accordance with the guidance in ASC Topic 480 &#8220;Distinguishing Liabilities from Equity.&#8221; Ordinary shares subject to mandatory redemption (if any) are classified as a liability instrument and are measured at fair value. Conditionally redeemable ordinary shares (including ordinary shares that feature redemption rights that is either within the control of the holder or subject to redemption upon the occurrence of uncertain events not solely within the Company&#8217;s control) are classified as temporary equity. At all other times, ordinary shares are classified as shareholders&#8217; equity. The Company&#8217;s ordinary shares feature certain redemption rights that are considered to be outside of the Company&#8217;s control and subject to the occurrence of uncertain future events. If it is probable that the equity instrument will become redeemable, we have the option to either (i) accrete changes in the redemption value over the period from the date of issuance (or from the date that it becomes probable that the instrument will become redeemable, if later) to the earliest redemption date of the instrument or (ii) recognize changes in the redemption value immediately as they occur and adjust the carrying amount of the instrument to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. The Company has elected to recognize the changes immediately.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000620" name="BKHAU:ConvertiblePromissoryNotesAndDerivativeLiabilitiesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_846_ecustom--ConvertiblePromissoryNotesAndDerivativeLiabilitiesPolicyTextBlock_zwBVcTYrTnRf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_86E_zV74ho4xCBLj">Convertible Promissory Notes and Derivative
Liabilities</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for convertible promissory
notes under ASC 470 &#8220;Debt&#8221; and evaluates embedded features under ASC 815 &#8220;Derivatives and Hedging&#8221;. Sponsor or
affiliate loans may be converted into ordinary shares upon completion of a Business Combination.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">If the conversion option has a fixed conversion
price and meets the &#8220;own-equity&#8221; scope exception in ASC 815-40, the note is accounted for as a single debt instrument. If
the conversion price is variable or indexed to the target company&#8217;s equity, the conversion feature is bifurcated and recorded as
a derivative liability, initially measured and subsequently remeasured at fair value each reporting period, with changes recognized in
earnings. The debt host is recorded at the residual amount and amortized to face value using the effective interest method. Convertible
notes and derivative liabilities are classified as current liabilities if settlement or conversion is expected within one year. Fair
value is estimated using the Black-Scholes, Binomial, or Monte Carlo models and is categorized as Level 3 under ASC 820.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>




</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000622" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zcBrJugEA9v2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><span id="xdx_866_zmUTTQRrcSgb">Recent Accounting Pronouncements</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Management does not believe that any recently issued, but not yet effective, accounting pronouncements, if currently adopted, would have a material effect on the Company&#8217;s financial statements.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>


</ix:nonNumeric></ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000624" name="BKHAU:ProposedPublicOfferingTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_ecustom--ProposedPublicOfferingTextBlock_zHlyaXh8pQkg" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 3 &#8212; <span><span id="xdx_82E_z9nY8IY4X5lk">Initial Public Offering</span></span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On March&#160;22, 2024, the Company sold <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_c20240301__20240322__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zXiUFe8kv7Sk" title="Sale of units in public offering"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-22_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000626" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">6,900,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units at a price of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_c20240322__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zYao8PhnnXE5" title="Sale of units per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-22_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000628" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit generating gross proceeds of $<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--GrossProceedsFromInitialPublicOffering_pp0p0_c20240301__20240322__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zitafqXrfNFk" title="Gross proceeds from Initial Public Offering"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:GrossProceedsFromInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-22_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000630" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">69,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. Each Unit consists of one Class A ordinary share and one-fifth (1/5) of one right (&#8220;Public Right&#8221;). Each Public Right will convert into one Class A ordinary share upon the consummation of a Business Combination.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000632" name="BKHAU:PrivatePlacementTextBlock"><p id="xdx_807_ecustom--PrivatePlacementTextBlock_ztx6vvhxXFGf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 4 &#8212; <span><span id="xdx_82E_zZ4Kr0R0FPCi">Private Placement</span></span></b></p>

The proceeds from the Private Units will be added to the proceeds from the Proposed Public Offering to be held in the Trust Account. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the proceeds from the sale of the Private Units will be used to fund the redemption of the Public Shares (subject to the requirements of applicable law), and the Private Units and all underlying securities will expire worthless.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On October&#160;16, 2023, the Company issued <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20231001__20231016__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember_z2uLLOj5QCCg" title="Number of shares issued, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-10-012023-10-16_custom_SponsorMember" id="Fact000644" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of Class B ordinary shares, $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--SharePrice_iI_c20231016__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zJSnB6MP9Vi1" title="Share Price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="AsOf2023-10-16_custom_SponsorMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000646" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.0001</ix:nonFraction></span> per share to the Sponsor (&#8220;Founder Shares&#8221;), for an aggregated consideration of $<span id="xdx_904_ecustom--AggregatedConsideration_c20231001__20231016__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_z51gf8T6GCMa" title="Aggregated consideration"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:AggregatedConsideration" contextRef="From2023-10-012023-10-16_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000648" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">25,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, or approximately $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SharePrice_iI_c20231016__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zCmYn7WFdCHc" title="Share Price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="AsOf2023-10-16_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000650" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.0145</ix:nonFraction></span> per share. <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Initial Shareholders have agreed, subject to certain limited exceptions, not to transfer, assign or sell any of their Founder Shares for a time period ending on the date that is the earlier of (A) six months after the completion of the Company&#8217;s initial business combination or (B) the date on which we complete a liquidation, merger, stock exchange or other similar transaction after our initial business combination that results in all of the public shareholders having the right to exchange their shares of ordinary shares for cash, securities or other property. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the converted shares of our Class A ordinary shares will be released from the lock-up if (1) the last reported sale price of the Company&#8217;s Class A ordinary shares equals or exceeds $12.00 per share (as adjusted for stock splits, stock capitalizations, reorganizations, recapitalizations and other similar transactions) for any 20 trading days within any 30-trading day period commencing at least 150 days after the initial business combination or (2) if the Company complete a transaction after the initial business combination which results in all of the shareholders having the right to exchange their shares for cash, securities or other property. The Initial Shareholders also agree not to transfer any ownership interest in, except to permitted transferees, their private placement until at least 30 days following the completion of the business combination. However, if after a business combination there is a transaction whereby all the outstanding shares are exchanged or redeemed for cash (as would be the case in a post-asset sale liquidation) or another issuer&#8217;s shares, then the Founder Shares or the private placement units (or any shares of ordinary shares thereunder) shall be permitted to participate.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Due from Related Party</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company reimbursed the Sponsor for its payment of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--ProfessionalFees_pp0p0_c20241201__20251130_zjDEFkKYesxe"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProfessionalFees" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000671" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">30,900</ix:nonFraction> </span>professional fees to a service provider which is no longer engaged by the Company. The amount was unsecured, interest-free and due on demand, which was offset with the repayment of the Promissory Note on March&#160;25, 2024. As of November&#160;30, 2025 and 2024, the Company had no amount due from related party.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Promissory Note &#8212; Related Party</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On October&#160;16, 2023, the Sponsor agreed to loan the Company up to an aggregate amount of $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_c20231016_pp0p0"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2023-10-16" id="Fact000672" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction> </span>to be used, in part, for transaction costs incurred in connection with the Proposed Public Offering (the &#8220;Promissory Note&#8221;). The Promissory Note is unsecured, interest-free and due on the earlier of: (i) September&#160;30, 2024 or (ii) the date on which the Company closes the IPO. The entire loan amount was repaid by the Company on March&#160;25, 2024. The Company had no borrowings under the Promissory Note as of November&#160;30, 2025 and 2024.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: left"><b>Convertible Notes &#8212; Related Party</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On June 13, 2025 and September 23, 2025, the Company issued convertible
promissory notes to the Sponsor (the &#8220;June Note&#8221; and the &#8220;September Note,&#8221; and collectively, the &#8220;Convertible
Notes&#8221;), <span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--ConvertiblePromissoryNotes_c20241201__20251130_zATDLgZVMfpb" title="Convertible promissory notes"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000674" name="BKHAU:ConvertiblePromissoryNotes">each permitting borrowings of up to $350,000. As of November 30, 2025, the Company had drawn $350,000 under the June Note
and $250,000 under the September Note.</ix:nonNumeric></span></p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23B_zcakJa6Vo8Lf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>



<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 67; Value: 1 -->
    <div id="xdx_23C_zgTY5O0HVeq9" style="border-bottom: Black 2pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">F-<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->16<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div>
    <div id="xdx_234_zJw8fXYc5TKk" style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_238_zjxZpMFmHS53" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The June Note bears interest at 6% per annum and the September Note bears interest at 10% per annum. The Convertible Notes are unsecured and mature on the earlier of (i) the consummation of a business combination or (ii) the Company&#8217;s liquidation date, as approved by stockholders. Upon consummation of a business combination, the Sponsor may elect to convert any unpaid principal and accrued interest into ordinary shares of the Company.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The conversion price was defined as the most favorable price per share, conversion rate, or valuation assigned to any equity securities issued by the target company in connection with the DeSPAC transaction to any third party during the thirty-six (36) months preceding conversion. Because the conversion price was variable and based on the valuation of equity securities issued by the target company, the embedded conversion feature did not qualify for the equity scope exception under ASC 815-40. Accordingly, the conversion feature was bifurcated and recorded as a derivative liability at fair value, with changes in fair value recognized in earnings.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On September 30, 2025, the Convertible Notes were
modified such that the conversion feature became convertible solely into the Company&#8217;s own ordinary shares at a fixed conversion
price of $1.00 per share, which represents one-tenth (1/10) of the Company&#8217;s $10.00 initial public offering price per unit. Following
the modification, the conversion option met the equity scope exception under ASC 815-40, and the Convertible Notes were accounted for
as debt with no further fair value remeasurement.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Upon the modification of the Convertible Notes
on September 30, 2025, the conversion feature was revised such that it met the equity scope exception under ASC 815-40. As a result, the
embedded derivative no longer required separate liability classification. The carrying amount of the derivative liability as of the modification
date was reclassified to additional paid-in capital.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">For the year ended November 30, 2025, the Company
recognized changes in fair value of the derivative liability through the date of modification. As of November 30, 2025, there was no derivative
liability outstanding.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">As of November&#160;30, 2025, the outstanding
principal balance under the Convertible Notes was $595,369, including unamortized discount of $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentCarryingAmount_iI_c20251130_zzWZEJhpzBs5" title="Unamortized discount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentCarryingAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000678" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,538</ix:nonFraction> </span>and accrued interest of $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--AccountsPayableAndAccruedLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_c20251130_zuTpZLcXsEol" title="Accrued interest"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AccountsPayableAndAccruedLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000680" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,454</ix:nonFraction></span>. There was no convertible note outstanding as of November 30, 2024.
</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="text-decoration: underline"><b>June Convertible Note</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">At issuance, the Company recorded a debt discount of $<span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--DebtDiscount_iI_c20251130_zynQOrsErho6" title="Debt discount"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:DebtDiscount" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000682" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,244</ix:nonFraction></span>, representing the fair value of the embedded conversion option.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">At September&#160;30, 2025, the derivative liability was measured at $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability_iI_c20250930_zNhNWsWr3iMa" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000684" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,177</ix:nonFraction></span>, compared to $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability_iI_c20250831_zH3Am8pSusvj" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-08-31" id="Fact000686" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,244</ix:nonFraction></span> at issuance, resulting in a derivative gain of $<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--NonCashLoss_iI_c20250831_zTHJXsocdrN" title="Non cash loss"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:NonCashLoss" contextRef="AsOf2025-08-31" id="Fact000688" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">67</ix:nonFraction></span> recognized in &#8220;Change in fair value of derivative liability.&#8221; Interest expense of approximately $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--InterestExpense_c20241201__20251130__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--JuneConvertibleNoteMember_zbKhPYQeGqD6" title="Interest expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpense" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-11-30_custom_JuneConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000690" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">9,838</ix:nonFraction></span> was incurred and accrued for the year ended November 30, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>&#160;</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the conversion feature was estimated
at the as converted value at September 30, 2025 and initial measurement date of June 13, 2025 to be $<span id="xdx_905_ecustom--FairValueOfConversion_c20241201__20250930__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--ConvertibleNoteMember_z0e7I3DJjNvh" title="Fair value of the conversion"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:FairValueOfConversion" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-09-30_custom_ConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000692" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,177</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_909_ecustom--FairValueOfConversion_c20241201__20250613__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--ConvertibleNoteMember_zyZXmexMkpy4" title="Fair value of the conversion"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:FairValueOfConversion" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-06-13_custom_ConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000694" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,244</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively. The
binomial tree model was used based on the following key assumptions:</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="text-decoration: underline"><b>September Convertible Note</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">At issuance, the Company recorded a debt discount of $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--DebtDiscount_iI_c20250930_zE5idDSoPW09" title="Debt discount"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:DebtDiscount" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000737" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,387</ix:nonFraction></span>, representing the fair value of the embedded conversion option related to the $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_c20250930_zWS7H7X7VKP5" title="Principal amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000739" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">150,000</ix:nonFraction></span> funded principal amount.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">At September 30, 2025, the derivative liability was measured at $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability_iI_c20250930__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--ConvertibleNoteMember_zOqFLqHWXoW2" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30_custom_ConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000741" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,361</ix:nonFraction></span>, compared to $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability_iI_c20250831__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--ConvertibleNoteMember_zCUKsAUQbke3" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeFairValueOfDerivativeLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-08-31_custom_ConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000743" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,386</ix:nonFraction></span> at issuance, resulting in a non-cash gain of $<span id="xdx_903_ecustom--NonCashGain_iI_c20250831_zcaN536dFgM5" title="Non cash gain"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:NonCashGain" contextRef="AsOf2025-08-31" id="Fact000745" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">25</ix:nonFraction></span> recognized in &#8220;Change in fair value of derivative liability.&#8221; Interest expense of approximately $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--InterestExpense_c20241201__20251130__us-gaap--ShortTermDebtTypeAxis__custom--ConvertibleNoteMember_zpdvsHSFIuR3" title="Interest expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpense" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-11-30_custom_ConvertibleNoteMember" id="Fact000747" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">5,616</ix:nonFraction></span> was incurred and accrued for the year ended November 30, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the conversion feature was estimated
at the as converted value at September 30, 2025 and initial measurement date of September 23, 2025 to be $<span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--FairValueOfConversion_c20241201__20250930_zsdpg0x7r3ci" title="Fair value of the conversion"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:FairValueOfConversion" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-09-30" id="Fact000749" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,361</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_907_ecustom--FairValueOfConversion_c20241201__20250923_zuFBJKOUgyDl" title="Fair value of the conversion"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:FairValueOfConversion" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-09-23" id="Fact000751" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,386</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively.

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Related Party Loans</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">In addition, in order to finance transaction costs in connection with an intended initial Business Combination, the Initial Shareholders or their affiliates may, but are not obligated to, loan us funds as may be required. If the Company completes an initial Business Combination, it will repay such loaned amounts. In the event that the initial Business Combination does not close, the Company may use a portion of the working capital held outside the Trust Account to repay such loaned amounts but no proceeds from the Trust Account would be used for such repayment. Certain amount of such loans may be converted into private at $10.00 per share at the option of the lender. As of November&#160;30, 2025 and the Company had <span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--WorkingCapitalLoans_iI_do_c20251130_zcMX0ov7wi1k" title="Working capital loans"><span id="xdx_902_ecustom--WorkingCapitalLoans_iI_do_c20241130_zZTzFeDBlpb5" title="Working capital loans"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:WorkingCapitalLoans" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000793" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:WorkingCapitalLoans" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-30" id="Fact000795" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> borrowings under the working capital loans.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Administrative Services Agreement</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company entered into an Administrative Services Agreement with the Sponsor on December&#160;4, 2023, commencing on the effective date of the registration statement of IPO through the later of the Company&#8217;s consummation of a Business Combination or 21 months from such effective date, to pay the Sponsor a total of $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--OtherGeneralAndAdministrativeExpense_c20241201__20251130_zy19A3IX3oLc" title="General and administrative expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherGeneralAndAdministrativeExpense" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000799" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">10,000</ix:nonFraction> </span>per month for office space and administrative and support services. The Company incurred and paid to the Sponsor $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--AdministrativeFeesExpense_pp0p0_c20241201__20251130_zXQZJnb4AJG5" title="Administrative expense paid to the Sponsor"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AdministrativeFeesExpense" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000801" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">120,000</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--AdministrativeFeesExpense_pp0p0_c20231201__20241130_zhVZ6dNLMlx8" title="Administrative expense paid to the Sponsor"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AdministrativeFeesExpense" contextRef="From2023-12-012024-11-30" id="Fact000803" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">83,945</ix:nonFraction></span> for the year ended November 30, 2025 and 2024, respectively; no amount was outstanding as of November&#160;30, 2025 and 2024.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>



</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000805" name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_809_eus-gaap--CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock_zgQzlxWA538l" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 6 &#8212; <span id="xdx_824_zfw5MCTTXN5d">Commitments and Contingencies</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Registration Rights</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The holders of the Founder Shares issued and outstanding on the date of this prospectus, as well as the holders of the private units and any shares of the Company&#8217;s insiders, officers, directors or their affiliates may be issued in payment of working capital loans and extension loans made to the Company (and any shares of ordinary shares issuable upon conversion of the underlying the private rights), will be entitled to registration rights pursuant to an agreement to be signed prior to or on the effective date of the registration statement. The holders of a majority of these securities are entitled to make up to two demands that we register such securities. The holders of the majority of the Founder Shares can elect to exercise these registration rights at any time commencing three months prior to the date on which these shares of ordinary shares are to be released from trust. The holders of a majority of the private units and units issued in payment of working capital loans made to us can elect to exercise these registration rights at any time commencing on the date that the Company consummate an initial business combination. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect to registration statements filed subsequent to the consummation of an initial business combination. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection with the filing of any such registration statements.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Right of First Refusal</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company has granted EF Hutton for a period of 18 months after the date of the consummation of the Company&#8217;s Business Combination, an irrevocable right of first refusal to act as lead left book-running managing underwriter or lead left placement agent with at least 50% of the economics; or, in the case of a three-handed deal, 40% of the economics, for any and all future public and private equity, convertible and debt offerings.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Underwriting Agreement</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company granted EF Hutton, the representative of the underwriters, a 45-day option from March&#160;22, 2024, to purchase up to <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_c20241201__20251130_zwBX9qEhJvV7" title="Sale of units in initial public offering"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000807" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,035,000</ix:nonFraction></span> additional Units to cover over-allotments, if any, at the IPO price less the underwriting discounts and commissions. The over-allotment option is deemed to be a freestanding financial instrument indexed on the contingently redeemable shares and is accounted for as a liability pursuant to ASC 480. The value of the over-allotment option was estimated to be $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueStockOptionsExercised_c20240301__20240322__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zBpBRMcD97o6" title="Option exercised"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueStockOptionsExercised" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-22_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000809" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">93,150</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March&#160;22, 2024. The underwriters did not excise the over-allotment option, as such, there was <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_pp0p0_do_c20251130_zwrUKYnYrCL2" title="Accrued liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000811" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></span> liability accrued on the balance sheet as of November&#160;30, 2025.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The underwriters were paid a cash underwriting discount of 1.0% of the gross proceeds of the IPO or $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering_c20241201__20251130__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__custom--ProposedPublicOfferingMember_zKwpJ4RF8dB6" title="Gross proceeds from proposed Public Offering"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="From2024-12-012025-11-30_custom_ProposedPublicOfferingMember" id="Fact000813" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">690,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. In addition, the underwriters will be entitled to a deferred fee of 3.5% of the gross proceeds of the IPO or $<span id="xdx_909_ecustom--GrossProceedsFromInitialPublicOffering_pp0p0_c20241201__20251130_zuRIRDzRACp6" title="Gross proceeds from Initial Public Offering"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:GrossProceedsFromInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" id="Fact000815" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" scale="0" unitRef="USD">2,415,000</ix:nonFraction></span> will be paid upon the closing of a Business Combination from the amounts held in the Trust Account, subject to the terms of the underwriting agreement.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Additionally, the Company issued the underwriters <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--SharesIssued_iI_c20240322__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zDHPlYnk6jvd" title="Additional shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-22_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000817" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">69,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of Class A ordinary shares for the representative shares, at the closing of the IPO as part of representative compensation on March&#160;22, 2024.</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000821" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_804_eus-gaap--StockholdersEquityNoteDisclosureTextBlock_z7ZdI2F7Ufvb" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 7 &#8212; <span id="xdx_82F_ztPwd5qGuk93">Shareholders&#8217; Deficit</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>Ordinary Shares</i></b> &#8212; The Company is authorized to issue up to <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesAuthorized_iI_c20251130__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassAMember_zRudyjJxJdt7"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30_us-gaap_CommonClassAMember" id="Fact000822" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">450,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Class A ordinary shares and <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesAuthorized_iI_c20251130__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassBMember_zEjBldNEnux5"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30_us-gaap_CommonClassBMember" id="Fact000823" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">50,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Class B ordinary shares, par value $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare_iI_c20251130__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassBMember_zPH2Ul9Etrd8"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30_us-gaap_CommonClassBMember" id="Fact000824" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.0001</ix:nonFraction></span> per share. Holders of Class A ordinary shares and holders of Class B ordinary shares are entitled to one vote for each share held on all matters to be voted on by the shareholders, except as required by law; provided that, prior to the initial Business Combination, only holders of our Class B ordinary shares will have the right to vote on the appointment of directors, and holders of a majority of the Class B ordinary shares may remove a member of the board of directors. With respect to any other matter submitted to a vote of the Company&#8217;s shareholders, including any vote in connection with the initial Business Combination, except as required by law or the Company&#8217;s articles of association, holders of Class A ordinary shares and holders of Class B ordinary shares will vote together as a single class. The Class B ordinary shares held by the Sponsor may convert into Class A ordinary shares at any time at their option, but will automatically convert into Class A ordinary shares upon the completion of the initial Business Combination on a one-for-one basis, subject to adjustments. On March&#160;18, 2024, the Company elected to convert <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ConversionOfStockSharesConverted1_c20240301__20240318__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassBMember_zaVnid5U0Thf"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConversionOfStockSharesConverted1" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-18_us-gaap_CommonClassBMember" id="Fact000825" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,725,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Class B ordinary shares into <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--ConversionOfStockSharesConverted1_c20240301__20240318__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassAMember_zLJFISoEIen6"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConversionOfStockSharesConverted1" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-18_us-gaap_CommonClassAMember" id="Fact000826" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,725,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Class A ordinary shares upon the closing of IPO. On March&#160;20, 2024, the Company and the Sponsor entered into the Second Amendment to the Subscription Agreement, pursuant to which the purchased amount of shares was adjusted to <span id="xdx_901_ecustom--AdjustedClassBOrdinaryShares_c20240301__20240320_zJDaGrYVKUc3" title="Adjusted class B ordinary shares"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:AdjustedClassBOrdinaryShares" contextRef="From2024-03-012024-03-20" id="Fact000828" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,983,750</ix:nonFraction></span> Class B ordinary shares, $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_c20240320_zGOY49IjINrg"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-20" id="Fact000829" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.0126</ix:nonFraction></span> par value per ordinary share. As of November&#160;30, 2025 and 2024, there were <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_c20251130__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonClassAMember_zMFcaElzWnO8"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30_us-gaap_CommonClassAMember" id="Fact000830" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">2,029,500</ix:nonFraction></span> Class A non-redeemable ordinary shares issued and outstanding (excluding 2,124,077 and 6,900,000 Class A ordinary shares subject to redemption as of November&#160;30, 2025 and November&#160;30, 2024, respectively).</p>

<p style="margin: 0; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</p>

<p style="margin: 0; font-size: 10pt; text-align: justify"><b><i>Rights </i></b>&#8212; Each holder of a right will receive one share of Class A Ordinary Share upon consummation of a Business Combination, even if the holder of such right redeemed all shares held by it in connection with a Business Combination. No fractional shares will be issued upon conversion of the rights. No additional consideration will be required to be paid by a holder of rights in order to receive its additional shares upon consummation of a Business Combination, as the consideration related thereto has been included in the Unit purchase price paid for by investors in the IPO. If the Company enters into a definitive agreement for a Business Combination in which the Company will not be the surviving entity, the definitive agreement will provide for the holders of rights to receive the same per share consideration the holders of the common stock will receive in the transaction on an as-converted into common stock basis and each holder of a right will be required to affirmatively covert its rights in order to receive one share underlying each right (without paying additional consideration). The shares issuable upon conversion of the rights will be freely tradable (except to the extent held by affiliates of the Company).</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">If the Company is unable to complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period and the Company liquidates the funds held in the Trust Account, holders of rights will not receive any of such funds with respect to their rights, nor will they receive any distribution from the Company&#8217;s assets held outside of the Trust Account with respect to such rights, and the rights will expire worthless. Further, there are no contractual penalties for failure to deliver securities to the holders of the rights upon consummation of a Business Combination. Additionally, in no event will the Company be required to net cash settle the rights. Accordingly, holders of the rights might not receive the shares of common stock underlying the rights.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000832" name="BKHAU:DueToTargetCompanyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_804_ecustom--DueToTargetCompanyTextBlock_z2iq8G644MU7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 8 &#8212; <span id="xdx_82D_z3SxobqmLPI3">Due to Target Company</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In connection with the Extension, the
Vesicor agreed to contribute half the Extension Payment. For the year ended November 2025 and 2024, Vesicor contributed $<span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--DueToTargetCompany_iI_c20251130_ziuN4is6mxnf" title="Due to target company"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:DueToTargetCompany" contextRef="AsOf2025-11-30" id="Fact000834" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">575,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
and $<span id="xdx_906_ecustom--DueToTargetCompany_iI_c20241130_z2wkEPbNS498" title="Due to target company"><ix:nonFraction name="BKHAU:DueToTargetCompany" contextRef="AsOf2024-11-30" id="Fact000836" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></span>,
respectively. The amount provided is interest free and will be reimbursed by the surviving company&#8217;s common stock upon closing
of the business combination. The loan can be converted at $9.40 per share. If, 180 days after closing, the company&#8217;s common stock is trading at $5.00 or lower,
the reimbursement rate will increase to $5.00 per share, with additional ordinary shares issued to compensate the Target for the monetary
value paid.</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000840" name="us-gaap:FairValueDisclosuresTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80D_eus-gaap--FairValueDisclosuresTextBlock_zqEoXE8u8Zn1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify"><b>Note 9 &#8212; <span id="xdx_827_z4InJ0l0oage">Fair Value Measurements</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s financial assets and liabilities reflects management&#8217;s estimate of amounts that the Company would have received in connection with the sale of the assets or paid in connection with the transfer of the liabilities in an orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement date. In connection with measuring the fair value of its assets and liabilities, the Company seeks to maximize the use of observable inputs (market data obtained from independent sources) and to minimize the use of unobservable inputs (internal assumptions about how market participants would price assets and liabilities). The following fair value hierarchy is used to classify assets and liabilities based on the observable inputs and unobservable inputs used in order to value the assets and liabilities:</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<table border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse">
  <tr>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; width: 0.25in; text-align: justify">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; width: 0.75in; text-align: justify">Level 1:</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">Quoted prices in active markets for identical assets or liabilities. An active market for an asset or liability is a market in which transactions for the asset or liability occur with sufficient frequency and volume to provide pricing information on an ongoing basis.</td> </tr>
  <tr>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td> </tr>
  <tr>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">Level 2:</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">Observable inputs other than Level 1 inputs. Examples of Level 2 inputs include quoted prices in active markets for similar assets or liabilities and quoted prices for identical assets or liabilities in markets that are not active.</td> </tr>
  <tr>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: middle; text-align: justify; vertical-align: middle">&#160;</td> </tr>
  <tr>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">&#160;</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">Level 3:</td>
    <td style="vertical-align: top; text-align: justify">Unobservable inputs based on our assessment of the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability.</td> </tr>
  </table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>


<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The following table presents information about the Company&#8217;s assets that are measured at fair value on a recurring basis as of November 30, 2025 and 2024, and indicate the fair value hierarchy of the valuation inputs the Company utilized to determine such fair value.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

  <tr style="vertical-align: bottom; background-color: White">
    <td style="text-indent: -0.125in; padding-left: 0.125in; vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><span id="xdx_8B2_zR1SPqO82Qnh" style="display: none">Schedule of Assets measured at fair value on a recurring basis</span></td>
    <td>&#160;</td>
    <td style="text-align: left">&#160;</td>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000861" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_805_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_zQRaYOhxfIul" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><b>Note 10 &#8212;&#160;<span id="xdx_820_zgdFiJp17Nsc">Segment Information</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">ASC Topic 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; establishes standards for companies to report in their financial statement information about operating segments, products, services, geographic areas, and major customers. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker, or group, in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s chief operating decision
maker has been identified as the Chief Executive Officer (&#8220;CODM&#8221;), who reviews the operating results for the Company as
a whole to make decisions about allocating resources and assessing financial performance. Accordingly, management has determined
that the Company only has&#160;one&#160;operating and reportable segment.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The CODM assesses performance for the single
segment and decides how to allocate resources. The measure of segment assets is reported on the balance sheet as total assets. When evaluating
the Company&#8217;s performance and making key decisions regarding resource allocation the CODM reviews several key metrics, which include
the following:</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>



<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-12-01to2025-11-30" escape="true" id="Fact000863" name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfSegmentReportingInformationBySegmentTextBlock"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" id="xdx_896_eus-gaap--ScheduleOfSegmentReportingInformationBySegmentTextBlock_zeU1BvyGFyI6" summary="xdx: Disclosure - Segment Information (Details)" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%">
